% Benedictory stanza.
% K3 ins. before the st.: flourish om namah srivighna-
% vinasanaya; then follow:
01*0001_01 jayati parAzarasUnuH satyavatIhRdayanandano vyAsaH
01*0001_02 yasyAsyakamalakoze vAGmayam amRtaM pibati lokaH
01*0002_01 dharmadRDhabaddhamUlo vedaskandhaH purANazAkhADhyaH
01*0002_02 kratukusumo mokSaphalo jayati kalpadrumo viSNuH
01*0003_01 pitAmahAdyaM pravadanti SaSThaM; maharSim akSayyavibhUtiyuktam
01*0003_02 nArAyaNasyAMzajam ekaputraM; dvaipAyanaM vedanidhiM namAmi
01*0004_01 pArAzaryavacassarojam amalaM gItArthagandhotkaTaM
01*0004_02 nAnAkhyAnakakesaraM harikathAsaMbodhanAbodhitam
01*0004_03 loke sajjanaSaTpadair aharahaH pepIyamAnaM mudA
01*0004_04 bhUyAd bhAratapaGkajaM kalimalapradhvaMsi naH zreyase
% D14 ins. sriganesaya namah | srilaksminrsimhaya namah
% before the st.; after the st. it ins. 1*, which is
% followed by:
01*0005_01 yaH zvetatvam upAgataH kRtayuge tretAyuge raktatAM
01*0005_02 yugme yaH kapilaH kalau ca bhagavAn kRSNatvam AyAga *
01*0005_03 * * * * * * * danti munayo yo yogibhir gIyate
01*0005_04 sa brahmA yadi vA haro yadi zivo yaH ko 'pi tasmai namaH
% T1 (damaged) ins. before the st.: srigurubhyo
% namah, which is followed by:
01*0006_01 vAgIzAdyAH sumanasaH sarvArthAnAm u * * *
01*0006_02 * * tvA kRtakRtyAH syus taM namAmi gajAnanam
% Then follow 13* and 12* (both fragmentary); then
% 191* (cf. v.l. 1.2.242); then 1.2.242 and finally:
01*0007_01 vyAsaM vasiSThanaptAraM zakteH pautram akalmaSam
01*0007_02 parAzarAtmajaM vande zukatAtaM taponidhim
01*0008_01 acaturvadano brahmA dvibAhur a * * * *
01*0008_02 abhAlalocanaH zambhur bhagavAn bAdarAyaNaH

% namah | srimate ramanujaya namah; then follows:

% This passage, which in the footnotes to the con-
% stituted text is said to be ins. in G3 after 1.107.
% 24, is not an insertion peculiar to G3, but occurs, as
% a matter of fact, in all S MSS. and is nothing more
% than the S version of the account of the birth of
% Bhima. An irregularity in the collation of the
% S MSS. was responsible for the misleading remark
% in the footnotes mentioned above.

% a recast of our adhy. 119, incorporating the addi-
% tional matter contained in passages No. 71-72
% above, which latter are Northern interpolations.

% (lines 130-133) with a repetition of the last two
% stanzas of adhy. 119, a confirmation (if any were
% needed) of the fact that this adhy. is an interpola-

% This passage is really an anticipation (in a
% variant form) of our adhy. 175. All S MSS.
% hitherto examined read it in fact at both places,
% here (i.e. after adhy. 157) and in its proper place
% (i.e. after adhy. 174). The printed editions of
% the S recensions, however, generally avoid this
% (seemingly unnecessary) repetition by erroneously
% reading the adhy. only at its first occurrence in S
% MSS., namely, after adhy. 157; only the grantha
% edition (Sarfojirajapuram) repeats tha adhy. in its
% proper place, and is in this respect a more correct
% representative of the Southern tradition. - The
% remark at the end of the footnotes to adhy. 156 to
% the effect that passage No. 96 of this this App. is ins. in
% S (except G6) after adhy. 156 is erroneous and
% should be deleted.

01_120_0008 zrAntaH zrAntatanur nityaM tato brahmANam Agamat
01_120_0009 gatvA pitAmahaM devaM namaskRtvA ca pAvakaH
01_120_0010 vijJApayAm Asa tadA svazarIrasya vedanAm
01_120_0011 brahmovAca tadA jJAtvA dagdhvA khANDavasatrakam
01_120_0012 arogaH siddhatejAz ca bhaviSyasi na saMzayaH
01_120_0013 uktamAtre tadA tena brahmaNA parameSThinA
01_120_0014 khANDavaM dagdhukAmaH san gatvA dagdhuM pracakrame
01_120_0015 vanapAlais tadA devaiH zamito vAriNAgamat
01_120_0016 saptakRtvo vighAtaM ca vahan khANDavadAhakam
01_120_0017 tato brahmANam agamat punar jJApitavAn prabhum
01_120_0018 tenApi ca samAdiSTas tad dhi pANDaravigrahaH
01_120_0019 naranArAyaNau yau tu dvApare kRSNaphalgunau
01_120_0020 bhaviSyantau mahAtmAnau khANDavasya samIpataH
01_120_0021 tau prArthaya mahAzUrau sAhAyyaM te kariSyataH
% This is an abbreviated version of the Sve-
% taki episode interpolated in N after 1.215.11; cf.
% passage No. 118 above. Its occurrence here in the
% few MSS. (T3 G2-4) in which it is found is some
% what irrelevant. Here the king is called sveta
% but in passage No. 118 he is called Svetaki; while
% in the next passage (No. 121) the king's name is
% given as Svetaketu!

% After adhy. 225, S1 K0.1.4 ins. an addl. adhy.
% (which is the final adhy. of the parvan in these
% MSS.):
01_121=0000 janamejaya uvAca
01_121_0001 dadAha bhagavAn vahniH kimarthaM khANDavaM ca tat
01_121_0002 kasya vA vipriyaM kurvan kasya vA priyakAmyayA
01_121_0003 na hy alpe kAraNe vahnir nirbandhaM kartum arhati
01_121_0004 dahanaM prati viprendra khANDavasyeti me matiH
01_121=0004 vaizaMpAyana uvAca
01_121_0005 zRNu me bhAvito rAjan sarvam eva yathAtatham
01_121_0006 yannimittaM dadAhAgniH khANDavaM pRthivIpate
01_121_0007 babhUva rAjA rAjendra zvetaketur iti zrutaH
01_121_0008 ikSvAkUNAm atiratho yajvA vipuladakSiNaH
01_121_0009 jagrAha dIkSAM sa nRpas tadA varSasahasrakIm
01_121_0010 RtvigbhiH sahito dhImAn ayajaj janamejaya
01_121_0011 tasya satre tadA tasmin samAgacchan maharSayaH
01_121_0012 vedavedAGgavidvAMso brAhmaNAz ca sahasrazaH
01_121_0013 tatas tu Rtvijas tasya dhUmavyAkulalocanAH
01_121_0014 kAlena mahatA khinnAs tatyajus te narAdhipam
01_121_0015 tataH prasAdayAm Asa RtvijaH sa mahIpatiH
01_121_0016 cakSur vikalatAM prAptA na prasIduz ca tasya te
01_121_0017 tatas teSAm anumate vighnitas tu narAdhipaH
01_121_0018 satraM samApayAm Asa Rtvigbhir aparaiH saha
01_121_0019 satreNa mahatA cApi yayau svargam abhiSTutaH
01_121_0020 RtvigbhiH sahitaH sarvaiH sasadasyaiH samanvitaH
01_121_0021 satre papau havyavAhaH sahasraM parivatsarAn
01_121_0022 sa tena haviSA vahniH parAM tRptim agacchata
01_121_0023 na caicchad agnir AdAtuM havir anyasya kasya cit
01_121_0024 aruciz cAvizat tasya tato 'gAt sa pitAmaham
01_121_0025 sa pitAmaham AsAdya etadarthaM nyavedayat
01_121_0026 bhagavan paramA tRptiH kRtA vai zvetaketunA
01_121_0027 aruciz cAvizat tIvrA tAM na zaknomy apohitum
01_121_0028 tam abravIt tadA vahniM sarvabhUtapitAmahaH
01_121_0029 aruciM nAzayiSye te samayaM pratipadyase
01_121_0030 bravItu tam uvAcAtha brahmANaM havyavAhanaH
01_121_0031 abravIt taM tato brahmA khANDavaM tvaM daher yadi
01_121_0032 tatra hy aneke ripavaH surANAM nivasanty uta
01_121_0033 matprasAdAd ruciH samyak tava vahne bhaviSyati
01_121_0034 kAlaM ca kaM cit kSamatAM tatas tad dhakSyate bhavAn
01_121_0035 mAnuSye 'pi ca saMbhUtau naranArAyaNAv RSI
01_121_0036 tAbhyAM tvaM sahito dAvaM dhakSyase havyavAhana
01_121_0037 evam astv iti taM vahnir brahmANaM pratyabhASata
01_121_0038 saMbhUtau tau viditvA tu naranArAyaNAv RSI
01_121_0039 tadasya mahato rAjaMs tac ca vAkyaM svayaMbhuvaH
01_121_0040 anusmRtya jagAmAtha dAvaM dagdhuM ca havyavAT
01_121_0041 etat te kathitaM rAjan yathaitad abhavat purA
01_121_0042 dahanAd yatra mukto 'sau mayo nAgo 'tha zArGgakAH
01_121=0042 Colophon.
% This is a variant version of the Svetaki
% episode; cf. passages No. 118 and 120 above, and
% the notes thereon. It will be noticed that the
% episode is actually repeated (in variant forms) in
% the four MSS. S1 K0.1.4 of the Kasmiri version,
% a repetition faithfully reflected in the Bharataman-
% jari (Kavyamala ed., pp. 107 and 112)!
